Datum of gage was corrected on Feb. 7, 2022, from 1,094.92 ft above NGVD of 1929 (levels by Board of Water Supply, City of New York) to 1,094.92 ft above Mean Sea Level Datum of Board of Water Supply, City of New York (levels by Board of Water Supply, City of New York).

EAST BRANCH DELAWARE RIVER AT DOWNSVILLE NY, USGS site 01417000 (USGS-01417000), stream in Delaware County, New York, draining 372 square miles. Daily means from 1941-07-01 to 2026-10-12, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
